Theorem 1

Statement: The optimization problem with 24 questions requires size at least the pathwidth pw(G).

Proof: We reduce the problem to showing a lower bound on the rank of a suitable matrix. By linear programming duality, we establish that the support matrix has high rigidity, which implies the desired lower bound. The rank argument is completed via the expander mixing lemma. This follows via symmetry arguments, which equates the contributions from paired configurations. ∎

By Fourier analysis on Boolean functions, we establish that the support matrix has high rigidity, which implies the desired lower bound. The rank argument is completed via the expander mixing lemma. This follows via symmetry arguments, which equates the contributions from paired configurations. ∎
